Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ARKP07 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : AMBAR
Hangi Tablo için : STOK29
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ARK PRES MALZEME TANITMA KARTI
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo
Sub PrintLabel()
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 100
	sayfaEni = 138
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 92
	EtiketEni = 134
	EtiketlerArasi = 30 + EtiketBoyu
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket" & trim(STOK29E.EVRAKNO),sayfaBoyu,sayfaEni,"")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetScaleStep(0.1)
	res = pe1.SetPrintScale(0.7)

	solMargin = 1
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 1
	For i = 1 to STOK29T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK29T.KOD(i)) <> ""then
			Miktar = STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			StokKodu = STOK29T.KOD(i)
			EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(DOC.DIV(Miktar , KutuIciMiktar))
			KutuIciMiktar = STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			EtiketAdedi = 1
			for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				end if
				EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
				if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
					Ust = UstMargin
					if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					end if
				else
					Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
				end if
						res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)

			Next
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 40
	D2 = SolMargin + 40
	D3 = SolMargin + 90

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y2 = Ust + 17
'	Y2 = Ust + 23

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust - 1,solMargin + 1,22,40,logo, 114)

	satirAra = 6
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust,SolMargin, EtiketBoyu - 3, sagMargin-SolMargin)
	Alt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
'	res = pe1.Line(Y1,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.2)
	For i = 1 to 5
		res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + satirAra,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	Next
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + satirAra - 1,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	Y3 = pe1.GetTop
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.2)
	for i = 1 to 3
		res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 9,D2,0,D3 - D2)
	Next
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,D2,EtiketBoyu - Y3 + 2,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,D3,EtiketBoyu - Y3 + 2,0)
	res = pe1.Ellipse(Y3 + 8,(D2 + solMargin) / 2 - 7,14,14)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom + 1, solMargin + 5, 10, D1 - solMargin - 10, "Etiketsiz malzemeyi kullanmayýnýz")

	StokKodu = STOK29T.KOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
	Miktar = STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	Birim = STOK29T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)
	CHKodu = STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,"")
	Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK29E.TARIH)
	IrsaliyeNo = STOK29E.XIRSALIYENO
	PartiNo = STOK29T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 7, solMargin, 8, sagMargin - solMargin, "MALZEME TANITIM KARTI")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Y2 + 0.2, solMargin + 1, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, "MALZEME/PARÇA ADI")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D1 + 1, 8, sagMargin - D1 - 2, ": " & StokAdi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin + 1, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, "STOK KODU")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D1 + 1, 8, sagMargin - D1 - 2, ": " & StokKodu)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin + 1, 8, D1 - solMargin +10, "AMBALAJ ÝÇÝ MÝKTAR           :")
	input =  Inputbox("Koli içi miktarý giriniz.","Dinamo")
	res = pe1.Write(35.2, solMargin + 45, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, input & " " & Birim)

'msgbox pe1.gettop
'msgbox d1
'msgbox solmargin

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D1 + 1, 8, 35, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(Miktar,18,Doc.SigDig(Miktar)) & " " & Birim)

	IrsaliyeMiktari = GetIrsaliyeMiktari(StokKodu)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(35.2, pe1.GetRight-8, 8, D1 - solMargin - 11, "ÝRSALÝYE MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 1, pe1.GetRight + 1, 8, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(IrsaliyeMiktari, 18,Doc.SigDig(IrsaliyeMiktari)) & " " & Birim)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin + 1, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, "FÝRMA")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D1 + 1, 8, sagMargin - D1 - 2, ": " & CHAdi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin + 1, 8, D1 - solMargin - 2, "ÝRSALÝYE NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D1 + 1, 8, sagMargin - D1 - 2, ": " & trim(IrsaliyeNo))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solMargin + 1, 8, sagMargin - solMargin - 2, "* Giriþ Kalite Kontrol Sorumlusu tarafýndan doldurulacak.")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(Y3 + 0.5, D2 + 1, 5, D3 - D2 - 2, "HAMMADDE PARTÝ NO")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, D2 + 1, 5, D3 - D2 - 2, "KONTROLÜ YAPAN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, D2 + 1, 5, D3 - D2 - 2, "ÝMZA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, D2 + 1, 5, D3 - D2 - 2, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.Write(Y3 + 0.5, D3 + 1, 5, sagMargin - D3 - 2, "ISKARTA NEDENÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Y3 + 4, D2 + 1, 6, D3 - D2 - 2, PartiNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(Alt + 0.2, solMargin + 1, 4, 40, "Form No : 01.01 - FR20")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, 52, "Yayýn Tarihi : 30/07/2004")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, 23, "Rev.No : 01")
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 4, 30, "Rev.Tarihi : 17/09/2004")

End Sub

Function GetIrsaliyeMiktari(StokKodu)
	for i = 1 to STOK29T.GetRecCount()
		if STOK29T.KOd(i) = StokKodu then
			Miktar = Miktar + STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		End if
	Next
	GetIrsaliyeMiktari = Miktar
End Function
Function MamulleriGetir(HammaddeKodu)
	Mamuller = ""
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01T","BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE",HammaddeKodu)
	if rows > 0 then
		Set T_BOMU01T = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01T_SELECT")
		T_BOMU01T.GroupBy("BOMREC_CODE")
		for i=1 to T_BOMU01T.GetRecCount
			Mamuller = Mamuller & trim(T_BOMU01T.BOMREC_CODE(i)) & ", "
		Next
	End If

	if len(Mamuller) > 2 then
		Mamuller = Left(Mamuller,len(Mamuller)-2)
	end if
	MamulleriGetir = Mamuller
End Function

Public ustmargin, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, sagmargin, altmargin
Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1

	Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res=pe1.Open(0,"Stok Kartlarý",-1,-1,"LANDSCAPE")
	sayfaboyu=210
	sayfaeni=297
	ustmargin=10
	altmargin=sayfaboyu-10
	solmargin=10
	satiryuksekligi=5
	sagmargin=sayfaeni-10
	
	Call xxx(pe1)
	
	res=pe1.Show()
	res=doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub xxx(pe1)
	
	res=pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
	sol=solmargin+7
	ust=ustmargin+15
	gen=160
	sol1=sol+gen+10
	gen1=90
	
	ust2=ust+35

	res=pe1.box(ustmargin, solmargin, altmargin-ustmargin, sagmargin-solmargin)
		
	res=pe1.write(ust-7, sol, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Ýmalatçý Detayý") 
	res=pe1.box(ust-2, sol-2, satiryuksekligi*5+2, gen)
	res=pe1.write(ust, sol, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Ýmalatçý Kodu") 
	res=pe1.write(ust, sol+70, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Bugünün Tarihi") 

	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*1, sol, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Ýmalatçý Adý") 
	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*2, sol, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Adresi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*3, sol, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Kontak Kiþi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*4, sol, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Telefon") 
	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*4, sol+45, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Fax:") 
	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*4, sol+75, satiryuksekligi, 40, "e-mail") 

	for i=0 to 3
		res=pe1.line((ust-1+satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi*i, sol-2, 0, gen)
	next
'********parça numarasý
	res=pe1.box(ust-2, sol1-2, satiryuksekligi*4+2, gen1)
	res=pe1.write(ust, sol1, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Prefix") 
	res=pe1.write(ust, sol1+30, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Base") 
	res=pe1.write(ust, sol1+55, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Suffix") 

	res=pe1.write(ust+satiryuksekligi*3, sol1, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Parça Adý") 
	for i=0 to 2
		res=pe1.line((ust-1+satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi*i, sol1-2, 0, gen1)
	next

' ******	paketleme bilgisi
	res=pe1.write(ust2-7, sol1, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Paketleme Bilgisi") 

	res=pe1.box(ust2-2, sol1-2, satiryuksekligi*20+2, gen1)
	for t=1 to 9
		res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*(t-1), sol1+3, satiryuksekligi, 15, t)
	next 

	res=pe1.write(ust2, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Paket Tipi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Devreye Giriþ Tarihi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*2, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Kutu Ýçi Adedi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*3, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Kutu Uzunluk") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*4, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Kutu Geniþlik") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*5, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Kutu Yüksekligi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*6, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Boþ Koli Aðýrlýðý(kg)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*7, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Parça Aðýrlýðý(kg)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*8, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Toplam Aðýrlýk(kg)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*10, sol1+2, satiryuksekligi, 80, "Dönüþümsüz Paket Detaylarý (Ýthal Parçalar Ýçin)") 


	for i=0 to 8
		res=pe1.line((ust2-1+satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi*i, sol1-2, 0, gen1)
	next

	for t=11 to 19
		res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*t, sol1+3, satiryuksekligi, 15, t)
	next 



	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*11, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Birim Kutudaki Parça Adedi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*12, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Layerdeki Kutu Adedi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*13, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Paletteki Toplam Layer Adedi") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*14, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Karton Kutu Uzunluk(cm)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*15, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Karton Kutu Geniþlik(cm)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*16, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Karton Kutu Yükseklik(cm)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*17, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Palet Uzunluk(cm)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*18, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Palet Geniþlik(cm)") 
	res=pe1.write(ust2+satiryuksekligi*19, sol1+10, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Palet Yükseklik(cm)") 

	for i=0 to 8
		res=pe1.line((ust2-1+satiryuksekligi*11)+satiryuksekligi*i, sol1-2, 0, gen1)
	next
	

End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ARKP07 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BOMU01
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Reçete Formu
Kod : 
Sub PrintForm()
	Set tableT = doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
	Set tableE = doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
	Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res=pe1.Open(0,"Reçete Listesi",-1,-1,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	satirsayisi = tableT.GetRecCount()
	satirb=95
	res = pe1.Write(15,12,10,188,doc.customp_gets("FNAMEL0"))	
	Call evrakbilgisi(pe1,tableE)
	For i=1 To satirsayisi
		Call satirbilgisi(pe1, satirsayisi, satirb,i,tableT)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub evrakbilgisi(pe1, TableE)
		'baþlýk
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",12)
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

		'evrak bilgileri -çerçeve çiz
	res=pe1.box(42,8,36,284)

		'evrak alan taným
	no=tableE.getfieldvalue("EVRAKNO",0)
	mamulkod=tableE.getfieldvalue("MAMULCODE",0)
	mamulad=tableE.getfieldvalue("GETSTRFIELD('STOK00', 'AD', MAMULCODE)", 0)
	miktar=formatnumber(tableE.getfieldvalue("MAMULMIKTAR",0),0,,,-1)
	birim=tableE.getfieldvalue("STOK_BIRIM",0)
	ap=tableE.getfieldvalue("AKTIF_PASIF",0)
	aciklama=tableE.getfieldvalue("ACIKLAMA",0)
	tarih=StrToNorDate(tableE.getfieldvalue("ELOG_LOGTARIH",0))

		' baþlýk yazdýr
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res=pe1.write(47,170,10,30,"Form")
	res=pe1.write(54,170,10,30,"Föy No")
	res=pe1.write(61,170,10,30,"Tarih")

	res=pe1.write(68,170,10,30,"Kullaným")
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",11)
	res=pe1.setbold(1)

	res=pe1.write(47,10,10,30,"Mamul Kodu")
	res=pe1.write(54,10,10,70,"Mamul Adý")
	res=pe1.write(61,10,10,30,"Baz Miktar")
	res=pe1.write(68,10,10,30,"Açýklama")
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res=pe1.setbold(0)

		'evrak alan yazdýr
	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(47,195,10,50,":"& trim("ÜRÜN AÐACI / BOMU01"))
	res=pe1.write(54,195,10,30,": " & trim(no))
	If ap="*" Then
	res=pe1.write(68,195,10,30,": " & trim("Aktif"))
	Else
	res=pe1.write(68,195,10,30,": " & trim("Pasif"))
	End If

	res=pe1.write(61,195,10,30,": " & trim(tarih))

	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",11)
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(47,35,10,30,": " & trim(mamulkod))
	res=pe1.write(54,35,10,95,":"&  trim(mamulad))
	res=pe1.write(61,35,10,30,": " & trim(miktar))
	res=pe1.write(61,55,10,10,birim)
	res=pe1.write(68,35,10,80,": " & trim(aciklama))

End Sub


Sub satirbilgisi(pe1, satirsayisi, satirb,i,tableT)

res=pe1.setbold(0)
res=pe1.selectfont("arial",10)

		'alanlarý oku
	sirano=tableT.getfieldvalue("BOMREC_SIRANO",i)
	fo=tableT.getfieldvalue("BOMREC_FO_OPTION",i)
	kaynaktipi=tableT.getfieldvalue("BOMREC_INPUTTYPE",i)
	kaynakkodu=tableT.getfieldvalue("BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE",i)
	kaynakadi=tableT.getfieldvalue("KAYNAK_AD",i)
	operasyon=tableT.getfieldvalue("BOMREC_OPERASYON",i)
	operasyonadi=tableT.getfieldvalue("OPERASTON_AD",i)
	miktar=tableT.getfieldvalue("BOMREC_KAYNAK0",i)
	birim=tableT.getfieldvalue("KAYNAK_BIRIM",i)

		'baþlýk yazdýr
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,10,7,8,"Sýra")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-1,10,7,8,"No")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,19,7,20,"FO/C")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,40,7,14,"Kaynak")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-1,42,7,14,"Tipi")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,65,7,48,"Kaynak Kodu")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,115,7,60,"Kaynak Adý")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,176,7,25,"Operasyon")
	res=pe1.write(satirb-7,201,7,50,"Operasyon Ad")
	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,10,7,4, sirano)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,19,7,20, fo)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,40,7,12, kaynaktipi)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,65,7,48, kaynakkodu)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,115,7,60, kaynakadi)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,176,7,15, operasyon)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,201,7,50, operasyonadi)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,251,7,30, miktar)
	res=pe1.write(satirb+i*7,281,7,15, birim)	
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public xSutun1, xSutun2, xSutun3, xSutun4, xSutun5, xSutun6, xSutun7, xSutun8, xSutun9, xSutun10
Public wSutun1, wSutun2, wSutun3, wSutun4, wSutun5, wSutun6, wSutun7, wSutun8, wSutun9, wSutun10
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip
Public AlanSayisi()		 
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
     Dim tableE, tableT
	ReDim AlanSayisi(100)		 
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	ReDim Secilenler(100,3)
'	Set xCizgi = Nothing
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0

      sayfaTuru = InputBox("Sayfa Türünü Giriniz" & Chr(13) & "A - A4, L - Letter (Sürekli Form)", "Dinamo", "A")

      Select Case UCase(sayfaTuru)
              Case "L"
                      sayfaBoyu = 279.4
                      sayfaEni = 215.9
              Case "A"
                      sayfaBoyu = 297
                      sayfaEni = 210
              Case Else
                      K = MsgBox("A veya L giriniz", 48, "Uyarý")
      End Select					
        Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

        	Set tableE = doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01E")
        	Set tableT = doc.GetTableObject("BOMU01T")
		Call Alanlar(TableE,TableT)
		If ToplamEn > 1.1 * SayfaEni Then
			k = SayfaBoyu
			l = SayfaEni
			SayfaEni = k
			SayfaBoyu = l
			Tip = "LANDSCAPE"
			res = pe1.Open(0, "Ürün Aðacý Listesi", SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, Tip)
		Else
			Tip = ""
			res = pe1.Open(0, "Ürün Aðacý Listesi", SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni , Tip)
		End If		

		BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
		satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 20

 		satirBaslangic = 70

		If doc.ErrorNumber Then
          	MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
			Exit Sub
        	End If
	Dim pe1
        siraNo = 0        
        Dim satirSayisi_tableT
        satirSayisi_tableT = tableT.GetRecCount()
        Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, tableE)
        For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
                Call Print_form_satir(pe1, tableT, I, tableE)
        Next
        
        Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1, tableE)
	   res = pe1.ReadDoc("c:\deneme.vpe")

        res = pe1.Show()
        res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, evrak)
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
        res = pe1.nopen()
        res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
        res = pe1.box(8, 8, 30, SayfaEni-110)
        res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
        res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
        res = pe1.setbold(1)
        res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
        res = pe1.write(15, 10, 10, SayfaEni - 75, "Ürün Aðacý")
        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		kBasla = 10
		kAra = 9
        res = pe1.write(kBasla, SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Form No         ")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 2* kAra , SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Revizyon No     ")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 3 * kAra, SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Revizyon Tarihi ")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 4 * kAra, SayfaEni - 62, 6, 50, "Föy No")
	   res = pe1.write(kBasla, SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": KF 04-01")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 2 * kAra , SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": 00")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 3 * kAra, SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": 01.07.1996")
        res = pe1.write(kBasla + 4 * kAra, SayfaEni - 32, 6, 50, ": " & trim(evrak.getfieldValue("EVRAKNO",1)))
        res = pe1.nopen()
        res = pe1.setbarcodeparms(2, 2)
        res = pe1.BarCode(kBasla + 4, SayfaEni - 62, kAra, 50, "CODE39", "KF 04-01", "")

        res = pe1.nopen()
		If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
	        res = pe1.Picture(12, 10, 15, 55, "\d24\images\muhtese.jpg", 50)
		Else
	        res = pe1.Picture(15, 10, 10, 35, "\d24\images\muhtese.jpg", 50)
		End If
        res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
        res = pe1.setbold(1)
        res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
'        res = pe1.write(15.8, 18, 10, 45, firma)
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		BBasla = 32
		SatirAra = 8
		StokKodu = evrak.GetFieldValue("MAMULCODE",1)
		res = pe1.write(BBasla, 12, 6, 25, "Stok Kodu")
        res = pe1.write(BBasla, 58, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & trim(StokKodu) & "-" & trim(doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,-1)))
		MamulMiktar = trim(evrak.GetFieldValue("MAMULMIKTAR",1))
		MamulMiktar = SayiBicimle(MamulMiktar,-1,0,1)
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, 12, 6, 50, "Ürün Aðacýna Esas Miktar")		
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + SatirAra, 58, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & MamulMiktar & " " & trim(doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,-1)))
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, 12, 6, 40, "Açýklama")		
		res = pe1.write(BBasla + 2 * SatirAra, 58, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & evrak.GetFieldValue("ACIKLAMA",1))
		
		
		Oran = BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn

        res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
			Sol = 12
			For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) / 3.22 * Oran
				Baslik = Grid(i,2)
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
					res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
					Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				End If
			Next

        res = pe1.pensize(0.6)

'köþedeki kutu
        res = pe1.Line(8, sayfaEni - 65, satirBaslangic - 22, 0)
        res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'        res = pe1.Line(46, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)

'yatay çizgiler
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
        res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-75)
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
        res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 2, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
        res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
        res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
        Dim satiryuksekligi, x
        satiryuksekligi = 6

        Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(ByVal pe1, ByVal tableT, ByVal satirNo, ByVal tableE)
Dim satirpozr, satiryuksekligi
        satiryuksekligi = 6
        sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
        If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
                Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1, tableE)
                siraNo = 1
                res = pe1.PageBreak()
                Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1, tableE)
                satirpozr = satirBaslangic
        Else
                If satirNo = 1 Then
                        satirpozr = satirBaslangic
                Else
                        satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
                End If
                siraNo = siraNo + 1
        End If
        
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
		FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
        res = pe1.setbold(0)
        Sol = 12
        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)  / 3.22 * BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
'				If DegiskenAdi = "" Then Exit For
				Degisken = trim(cstr(TableT.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Isnumeric(Degisken) And DegiskenAdi <> "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = DecimalHesapla(TableT,DegiskenAdi)
					Degisken = SayiBicimle(Degisken, Ondalik, 1, 1)
				Elseif IsDate(Degisken) Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
					res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
					Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
					xCizgi(j) =	Sol - 2
					j = j + 1
				End If	
			Next					 
        sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(ByVal pe1, ByVal evrak)

        res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'dýþ çerçeve
        res = pe1.box(8, 10, satirSonu - 3, SayfaEni - 20)

'dikey çizgiler
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) -1
		  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 19, 0)
		Next
'        Call imza(pe1)

'        Call Logo(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar(TableE,TableT)

	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","BOMU01T")
	ReDim Preserve Grid(UBound(Grid,1),UBound(Grid,2)+1)

	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Select Case trim(Grid(i,0))
			Case "BOMREC_SIRANO"
				Grid(i,2) = "Sýra No"
			Case "BOMREC_FO_OPTION" 
				Grid(i,2) = "FO/C"
			Case "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Kaynak Tipi"
			Case "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Kaynak Kodu"
			Case "KAYNAK_AD" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Kaynak Adý"
			Case "BOMREC_OPERASYON" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Operasyon"
			Case "OPERASTON_AD" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Operasyon Adý"
			Case "BOMREC_KAYNAK0" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Hammadde Miktarý veya Tezgah Süresi"
			Case "KAYNAK_BIRIM" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Birim"
			Case "BOMREC_PLANOPS" 
				Grid(i,2) = "PL ML OPT"
			Case "BOMREC_YMAMULPS" 
				Grid(i,2) = "PK NO"
			Case "BOMREC_YMAMULCODE" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Operasyon Sonu Üretilen Y.Mamul"
			Case "BOMREC_YMAMULPM" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Yarý Mamul Miktarý"
			Case "YMAMUL_BIRIM" 
				Grid(i,2) = "YM Birimi"
			Case "BOMREC_REPLACE_K_OPS" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Ana Hammadde Varyant Tanýmý"
			Case "BOMREC_REPLACE_C_OPS" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Ölçü Varyantý Tanýmý"
			Case "BOMREC_KAYNAK1" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Ayar Setup süresi"
			Case "BOMREC_KAYNAK2" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Yükleme Süresi"
			Case "KAYNAK_BIRIM" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Birim"
			Case "BOMREC_LOWERLIMIT" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Mamul Alt Limit"
			Case "BOMREC_UPPERLIMIT" 
				Grid(i,2) = "Mamul Üst Limit"
			Case "BOMREC_BATCH_UNIT" 
				Grid(i,2) = "BAT"
			Case "BOMREC_FLOORFLAG" 
				Grid(i,2) = "RND"
		End Select
	Next
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	ToplamEn = ToplamEn / 3.22
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
        res = pe1.setbold(0)
        res = pe1.setitalic(0)
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
        res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
        res = pe1.setrotation(0)
        res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
        res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
        res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub
Function BosMu(Table,Field)
	k=0
  	satir = Table.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To Satir
		AlanDegeri = trim(Table.GetFieldValue(Field,i))
		If Isnumeric(AlanDegeri) Then
			If  Alandegeri <> 0  Then
				k = k + 1
			End If
		Else
			If  AlanDegeri <> "" Then
				k = k + 1
			End If
		End If
	Next
	BosMu = k
End Function
Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function
Function SayiBicimle(Sayi, OndalikSayisi, SondaSifir, Ayirac)
    If OndalikSayisi >= 0 Then
        Sayi = Round(Sayi, OndalikSayisi)
    End If
    Nokta = InStr(1, Sayi, ".")
    If Nokta = 0 Then
        ondalik = ""
    Else
        ondalik = Right(CStr(Sayi), Len(CStr(Sayi)) - Nokta)
    End If
    TamSayi = CStr(Fix(Sayi))
    If SondaSifir > 0 Then
        ondalik = SifirEkle(ondalik, OndalikSayisi)
    ElseIf SondaSifir = -1 Then
        ondalik = ondalik
    Else
        ondalik = SifirSil(ondalik)
    End If
    If Ayirac = 1 Then
        TamSayi = VirgulEkle(TamSayi)
    End If
    If ondalik = "" Then
        SayiBicimle = TamSayi
    Else
        SayiBicimle = TamSayi & "." & ondalik
    End If
End Function
Function SifirEkle(Sayi, OndalikHane)
    Sayi = CStr(Sayi)
    If OndalikHane - Len(Sayi) > 0 Then
        SifirEkle = Sayi & String(OndalikHane - Len(Sayi), "0")
    Else
        SifirEkle = Sayi
    End If
End Function
Function SifirSil(Sayi)
    Sayi = Trim(CStr(Sayi))
    While Right(Sayi, 1) = "0"
        Sayi = Left(Sayi, Len(Sayi) - 1)
    Wend
    SifirSil = Sayi
End Function
Function VirgulEkle(Sayi)
    If Sayi = 0 Then Exit Function
    Sayi = CStr(Sayi)
    k = Len(Sayi)
    For i = 0 To k - 1
        If i > 0 And i Mod 3 = 0 Then
            Sagdan = "," & Sagdan
        End If
        Sagdan = Mid(Sayi, k - i, 1) & Sagdan
    Next
    VirgulEkle = Sagdan
End Function
Function DecimalSayisi(Sayi)
	sayi = Cstr(Sayi)
    Nokta = InStr(1, Sayi, ".")
    If Nokta = 0 Then
		DecimalSayisi = 0
    Else
		ondalik = Right(CStr(Sayi), Len(CStr(Sayi)) - Nokta)
		DecimalSayisi = len(SifirSil(Ondalik))
    End If
End Function
Function DecimalHesapla(Table,Degisken)
k = 0
DecCount = 0
	For i=1 To Table.GetRecCount()
		DecCount = DecimalSayisi(Table.GetFieldValue(Degisken,i))
		If DecCount >= k Then
			k = DecCount
		End If
	Next
DecimalHesapla = k
End Function

Sub PrintForm()
		Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
		res = pe1.Open(0, "Ürün Aðacý Listesi", -1, -1, "LANDSCAPE")
		res = pe1.WriteRTFFile(20,20,180,265,"d:\bel1.rtf")
'		Call WriteRTFDosya(pe1,20,20,180,265,"d:\bel1.rtf")
		res = pe1.Show()
		res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub WriteRTFDosya(pe1,r1,c1,yukseklik,en,Dosya)
	Set fs = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	Set Dosya1 = fs.OpenTextFile(Dosya,1,True)
	Yazi = Dosya1.ReadAll
	res = pe1.WriteRTF(r1,c1,yukseklik,en,Yazi)
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ARKP07 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : CARIRC
Hangi Tablo için : CARIRC
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Cari Hesap Adres Etiketleri
Kod : 
Public EndenEtiketSayisi,BoydanEtiketSayisi,EtiketEni, EtiketBoyu, EndenBosluk, BoydanBosluk
Public i,j, xOffset, yOffset, x, y

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, tableT, satirNo, tableE)
    
	cHKodu = tableT.GetFieldValue("KOD", satirNo)
	cHAdi = tableT.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('CARI00','AD',KOD)", satirNo)
	cHAd2 = tableT.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('CARI00','NAME2',KOD)", satirNo)
	
	Adi = doc.GetUDFValue("07",cHKodu,"ILGILI01",0)

	Adres1 = tableT.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('CARI00','ADRES_1',KOD)", satirNo)
	Adres2 = tableT.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('CARI00','ADRES_2',KOD)", satirNo)
	Adres3 = tableT.GetFieldValue("GETSTRFIELD('CARI00','ADRES_3',KOD)", satirNo)

	xAd = 42+ (x-1)*xOffset
	xAdres = 20+ (x-1)*xOffset

	SatirAraligi = 5
	ySayin = 14 + (y-1)*yOffset
	yAd = 20+ (y-1)*yOffset
	ySirketAdi = 29+ (y-1)*yOffset
	If trim(cHAd2) ="" Then
		ySirketAdi2 = ySirketAdi
	Else
		ySirketAdi2 = ySirketAdi + 6
	End If	
	yAdres1 = ySirketAdi2 + 7 
	yAdres2 = yAdres1 + SatirAraligi 
	yAdres3 = yAdres2 + SatirAraligi 

	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.Write(ySayin, xAd, 7, 55, "Sayýn")		
	res=pe1.selectfont("Arial",11)

	res=pe1.Write(yAd, xAd, 7, 55, Adi)		
	res=pe1.Write(ySirketAdi, xAdres, 7, 80, cHAdi)		
	If trim(cHAd2) <> "" Then
		res=pe1.Write(ySirketAdi2, xAdres, 7, 80, cHAd2)		
	End If
	
	res=pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.selectfont("Arial",10)
			
	res=pe1.Write(yAdres1, xAdres, 7, 80,Adres1)
	res=pe1.Write(yAdres2, xAdres, 7, 80,Adres2)
	res=pe1.Write(yAdres3, xAdres, 7, 80,Adres3)
End Sub

Sub FormOlustur(TableE, TableT)
On Error Resume Next
Dim pe1, sayfa
      Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
      res = pe1.Open(0,"Adresler",-1,-1,"")
		
		satirSayisi=tableT.GetRecCount()
		
		sayfaSayisi = basilacakSatir / (EndenEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketSayisi)
		If sayfaSayisi = fix(sayfaSayisi) Then
			sayfaSayisi = fix(sayfaSayisi) 
		Else
			sayfaSayisi = fix(sayfaSayisi) + 1
		End If
		
		xOffset = EtiketEni + EndenBosluk
		yOffset = EtiketBoyu + BoydanBosluk
				
        
			x=1
			y=1
			For i=1 To satirSayisi
				kod=tableT.GetFieldValue("KOD", i)				
				akt = tableT.getfieldvalue("SELECTED", i)
				If akt = "E" And trim(kod) <> "" Then
					res = pe1.nopen()
'					res = pe1.Picture(5 + (y - 1) * yOffset, 5 + (x - 1) * xOffset, EtiketBoyu, EtiketEni, "\d24\images\dinamo.jpg", 16)
					Call Print_form_satir(pe1, tableT, i, tableE)					
					sayi=sayi+1
					x=x+1							
					If x=EndenEtiketSayisi+1 Then
						x=1
						y=y+1
					End If							
				End If
				If y = BoydanEtiketSayisi+1  Then
				   res = pe1.PageBreak()
					x=1
					y=1
				End If
       Next
       res = pe1.Show()
       res = par.SetParameter("NOFOCUS", 1)
End Sub


Sub PrintForm()
        On Error Resume Next
        Dim tableE, tableT
		
		Call Etiket

        Set tableT = doc.GetTableObject("ADRESLER")
        
        If doc.ErrorNumber Then
                MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
                Exit Sub
        End If
        
        Call FormOlustur(tableE, tableT)
End Sub

Sub Etiket()
        EtiketTuru = InputBox("Sayfa Türünü Giriniz" & Chr(13) & "Enden Etiket Sayýsý x Boydan Etiket Sayýsý" & _
					Chr(13) & "(Örnek : 3x5 gibi)" , "Dinamo","2x7")

        EtiketBoyutlari = InputBox("Etiket Boyutlatýný Giriniz" & Chr(13) & "Etiket Eni(mm) x Etiket Boyu(mm)" & _
					Chr(13) & "(Örnek : 99x57 gibi)" , "Dinamo","99.1x38.1")

		Boy=Len(EtiketTuru)
		Carpi=instr(ucase(EtiketTuru),"X")
		EndenEtiketSayisi=cint(trim(Left(EtiketTuru,Carpi-1)))
		BoydanEtiketSayisi=cint(trim(Mid(EtiketTuru,Carpi+1,Boy-Carpi)))

		Boy=Len(EtiketBoyutlari)
		Carpi=instr(ucase(EtiketBoyutlari),"X")
		EtiketEni=cint(trim(Left(EtiketBoyutlari,Carpi-1)))
		EtiketBoyu=cint(trim(Mid(EtiketBoyutlari,Carpi+1,Boy-Carpi)))

		EndenBosluk = 2.5
		BoydanBosluk = 0
End Sub

Makro Kodu : ETIKET
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ford Sevkiyat Etiketi
Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Unvan, Adres, UreticiKodu, Grade
Sub PrintLabel()

	UreticiKodu = "ASDQA"
	Unvan = "ARK PRES EM.KEMERLERI SAN.T.AS"
	Adres = "VEZIRCIFTLIGI MEV.ORDULULAR MH YAVUZSELIM CD.NO:14/A 41270 KULLAR 41"
	Grade = "B"

	Hata = ""
	STOK60T.SafePack
	for i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)) <> "E" Then
			stokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
			CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
			CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu,CHKodu)
			KonteynerKodu = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			if trim(KonteynerKodu) = "" then
				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " konteyner kodu girilmemiþ." & vbcrlf
			end if
			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then
				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " sandýk içi miktar girilmemiþ." & vbcrlf
			end if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(Hata) <> "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(Hata & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	SevkAdresKodu = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	if trim(SevkAdresKodu) = "" then
		k = Doc.d7msgBox("Sevk adres kodu girilmemiþ","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 18
	EtiketBoyu = 117
	EtiketEni = 171
	EtiketlerArasi = 143
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO),sayfaBoyu,sayfaEni,"")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)

	solMargin = 20
	sagMargin = sayfaEni- 19
	CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	For i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) <> "" and trim(STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)) <> "E" then
			Miktar = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
			StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
			CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu,CHKodu)
			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 Then
				EtiketAdedi = 1
			Else
				EtiketAdedi = Doc.Div(Miktar, KutuIciMiktar)
			end if
			for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
				Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
				if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
					KutuMiktari = Kalan
				else
					KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
				end if
				for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten  tane
					EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
					if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
						Ust = UstMargin
						if EtiketNo <> 1 then
							res = pe1.PageBreak()
						end if
					else
						Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
					end if
					Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari)
				next
			Next
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo, KutuMiktari)
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 42
	D2 = SolMargin + 79
	D3 = SolMargin + 110
	D4 = SolMargin + 113
	D5 = SolMargin + 125
	D6 = SolMargin + 143

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 12.5
	Y2 = Ust + 24
	Y3 = Ust + 35
	Y4 = Ust + 45.5
	Y5 = Ust + 56
	Y6 = Ust + 66
	Y7 = Ust + 79
	Y8 = Ust + 88
	Y9 = Ust + 98
	Y10 = Ust + 117

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust,SolMargin, EtiketBoyu, sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D2,0,sagMargin-D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4,D5,0,sagMargin-D5)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6,D2,0,sagMargin-D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7,SolMargin,0,D2 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8,D2,0,sagMargin-D2)
	res = pe1.Line(Y9,SolMargin,0,sagMargin-SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Y5,D1,Y9-Y5,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust,D2,Y3-Ust,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5,D2,Y10-Y5,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D3,Y3-Y2,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8,D4,Y9-Y8,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust,D5,Y1-Ust,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3,D5,Y5-Y3,0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2,D6,Y3-Y2,0)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	Alici = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 1.5, SolMargin + 2.5,4,20,"RECEIVER / ALICI")
	if len(Alici) >= 22 then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",16)
	else
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",18)
	end if
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 4.5, SolMargin,8,D2 - solMargin,trim(Alici))

	sol = D2
	sag = D5
	rUst = Ust
	StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)
	CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu,CHKodu)
'	Kapi = STOK60T.DOK(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"DOCK / KAPI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",18)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,trim(Kapi))

	sol = D5
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Lokasyon = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_4",StokKodu,"")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"LOCATION / LOKASYON")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",18)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,Lokasyon)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y1
	IrsaliyeNo = "A-" & right("0" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO),6)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(IrsaliyeNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PACKING SLIP NO/IRSALIYE NO(N)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 5, sol,8,sag - sol,IrsaliyeNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+10,sol1,11,BarkodGen,"CODE39","N" & trim(IrsaliyeNo) & "","")

	sol = D2
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SUPPLIER NAME & ADDRESS / IMALATCI ADI & ADRESI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol + 1,4,sag - sol - 2,Unvan)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 8, sol + 1,4,sag - sol - 2,Adres)

	sol = D2
	sag = D3
	rUst = Y2
'	NetAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_3",StokKodu,"")	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"NET WEIGHT / NET AÐIRLIK (kg)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,NetAgirlik)

	sol = D3
	sag = D6
	rUst = Y2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	BrutAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",StokKodu,"")	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"GROSS WEIGHT / BRÜT AÐIRLIK (kg)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,BrutAgirlik)

	sol = D6
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y2
	KutuSayisi = 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"NO OF BOXES / KUTU SAYISI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,KutuSayisi)

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D5
	rUst = Y3
	sKod = replace(trim(CHStokKodu)," ","-")
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(sKod)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PART NO / PARÇA NUMARASI (P)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",18)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,8,sag - sol,sKod)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+10,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & sKod & "","")


	sol = D5
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y3
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"DANGER CODE / TEHLÝKE KODU")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",18)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,"")

	sol = D5
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y4
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"EXPIRATION DATE / SON KULLANMA TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",18)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,"")

	sol = solMargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y5
'	KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
'	if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = STOK60T.MIKTAR(satirNo)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KutuMiktari)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"QUANTITY / MÝKTAR (Q)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 5.5, sol,8,sag - sol,KutuMiktari)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+10.5,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & KutuMiktari & "","")

	sol = D1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y5
	Miktar = STOK60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)	
	SevkiyatMiktari = Miktar
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SevkiyatMiktari)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SHIPMENT QUANTITY / SEVKÝYAT MÝKTARI (T)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 5.5, sol,8,sag - sol,SevkiyatMiktari)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+10.5,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","T" & SevkiyatMiktari & "","")

	sol = D2
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y5
	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PART DESCRIPTION")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol + 1,8,sag - sol - 2,ParcaAdi)


	sol = solmargin
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y7
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(UreticiKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SUPPLIER / SEVKIYATCI (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3, sol,8,sag - sol,UreticiKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+8,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & UreticiKodu,"")

	sol = D1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y7
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"GRADE / DERECE")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",36)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol,17,sag - sol,grade)

	sol = D2
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y6
	KonteynerKodu = trim(Doc.GetdbFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_1",StokKodu,""))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(KonteynerKodu)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"CONTAINER CODE / KONTEYNER KODU (R)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4, sol,8,sag - sol,KonteynerKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	if trim(KonteynerKodu) <> "" then
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+8,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","R" & KonteynerKodu & "","")
	end if
	sol = D2
	sag = D4
	rUst = Y8
	SevkTarihi = Doc.StrToNordate(STOK60E.TARIH)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"DATE SHIPPED / SEVKÝYAT TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,SevkTarihi)

	sol = D4
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y8
	SevkTarihi = Doc.StrToNordate(STOK60E.TARIH)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ENG. CHANGE NO / MÜH. DEÐÝÞÝKLÝK NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 4.5, sol,8,sag - sol,"")
	
	sol = solMargin
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y9
	SeriNo = UreticiKodu & EtiketNo
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"SERIAL NO / SERÝ NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3.5, sol,8,sag - sol,SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+8,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo & "","")

	sol = D2
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y9
	PartiNo = trim(GetPartiNo(STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PartiNo)
	sol1 = sol + (sag - sol - BarkodGen) / 2
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"BATCH NUMBER / PARTÝ NUMARASI (H)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",11)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 3.5, sol,8,sag - sol,PartiNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	if trim(PartiNo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+8,sol1,10,BarkodGen,"CODE39","H" & PartiNo & "","")
	End if
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetPartiNo(StokKodu)
	for i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(StokKodu) = trim(STOK60T.KOD(i)) Then
			GetPartiNo = STOK60T.LOTNUMBER(i)
			Exit Function
		end if
	Next
End Function

Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	
	AraToplam = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 275
	sayfaEni = 213
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)

	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 70
	satirBaslangic = 85
	satirBaslangic = 107.5
	sonSayfasatirSonu = satirSonu - DipToplamSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi

	ustmargin = 15
	solmargin = 11
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0 
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK70T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S70T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S70 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	S70T.CopyTable(S70)
	S70T.SafePack
   S70T.groupby("KOD")   
	satirSayisi = S70T.GetRecCount()

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)

	For i = 1 To satirsayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	chkodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi  = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	adres1 = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
	adres2 = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
	adres3 = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)

'	vergidairesi = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
'	vergino = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)

	vergidairesi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", chkodu, "")
	vergino = doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", chkodu, "")

	tarih = doc.strtonordate(STOK70E.TARIH(1))
	satirSayisi = STOK70T.GetRecCount()

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	bBasla = ustmargin + 32
	res=pe1.write(bBasla, solmargin + 10, 5, 100, chadi)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 10, 5, 150, adres1)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 10, 5, 150, adres2)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 10, 5, 150, adres3)

' *************************************************************************
'	Nitelik = STOK70E.NITELIK
'	if trim(Nitelik) = "SR" then
'		res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
'		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+37, solmargin, 5, sagmargin - solMargin, "ALIM ÝADE FATURASI")		
'		res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
'		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	end if
' *************************************************************************

	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 59, solmargin + 10, 5, 50, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 59, solmargin + 60, 5, 50, vergino)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 28, solmargin +156, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXXYYYY=STOK70T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set SS70T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXXYYYY")
	Set SS70 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	SS70T.CopyTable(SS70)
	SS70T.SafePack
	SS70T.GroupBy("IRSALIYENO")



	For k = 1 To SS70T.getreccount
		irsaliyeno = SS70T.IRSALIYENO(k)
		irsaliyetarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeno, "")
		irsaliyetarihi = doc.strtonordate(irsaliyetarihi)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				D=d+1
				res = pe1.write(ustmargin+49+(d-1)*4, solmargin + 145, 5, 40, irsaliyetarihi)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				res = pe1.write(ustmargin+49+(d-1)*4, solmargin + 165, 5, 40, irsaliyeno)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
			
	Next
	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	
' **************************************************************
		If trim(S70T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub

		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

'		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
'			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'			siraNo = 1
'			res = pe1.PageBreak()
'			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
'			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
'		Else
'			If satirNo = 1 Then
'					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
'			Else
'					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
'			End If
'			siraNo = siraNo + 1
'		End If		
' ************************************************************************

		RSATIR = S70T.GetRecCount()
		If sonSatir >=  satirSonu or RSATIR and sonSatir >= sonSayfasatirSonu Then
			Call AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
				satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.setbold(0)

		if pe1.GetPageCount() > 1 and sirano = 1 then 
			res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr , SOLMARGIN + 130, satirYuksekligi, 20, "NAKLÝ YEKÜN") 
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, SOLMARGIN + 145, satirYuksekligi, 39, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,2)) 		
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			satirpozr = satirpozr + satirYuksekligi
			sirano = sirano + 1
		end if

		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = solMargin + 5

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				stokkodu = S70T.KOD(satirno)
				mstokkodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
				mstokadi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
				if degiskenAdi = "TL_FIYAT" then
						Degisken=doc.div(S70T.TL_TUTAR(satirno), S70T.SF_MIKTAR(satirno))
				else
						Degisken = trim(cstr(S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				end if

				if degiskenAdi = "TL_TUTAR" then
					TT = S70T.TL_TUTAR(SatirNo)
'					AraToplam = AraToplam + S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
					AraToplam = AraToplam + TT
				end if

				If trim(degiskenAdi) = "KOD" Then
						If trim(mstokkodu) <> "" Then
								Degisken = trim(mstokkodu) 
						End If
				End If
				If trim(degiskenAdi) = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
						If trim(mstokadi) <> "" Then
								Degisken = trim(mstokadi) 
						End If
				End If
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.Numformat(degisken, 20, Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				if i=0 or i=1 then
					res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				else
					res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				end if
				
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				xCizgi(j) =	Sol - 2
				j = j + 1
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub


Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
		
		For i = 0 To 3
			SutunG = Grid(i,1)
			Sol1 = Sol1 + SutunG
		Next
	Ondalik = Grid(4,6)

	Toplam = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM(1), 39, Ondalik)
	iskontotoplam = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1), 39, Ondalik)

	nettoplam = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1),39,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = STOK70T.KDV_ORANI(1)
	KOndalik = Doc.SigDig(KDVOrani)
	kdvc = doc.Numformat(KDVOrani,7, KOndalik)

	kdvtutar = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM(1),39,Ondalik)
	geneltoplam = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	geneltoplam = doc.Numformat(geneltoplam,39,Ondalik)
	not1 = STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3(1)

	sol1 = sol1 - 4
	satirs = 219

'	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs, sol1, 5, 39, iskontotoplam)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+8, sol1, 5, 39, nettoplam)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+16, sol1-10, 5, 10, kdvc)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+16, sol1, 5, 39, kdvtutar)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+24, sol1, 5, 39, geneltoplam)
'	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

' QQQ
	sol1 = sol1 + 12
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1+1, 5, 39, String(22, "-"))
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, Toplam)

	If iskontotoplam <> "" Then
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "ÝSKONTO")
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, iskontotoplam)
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1+1, 5, 39, String(22, "-"))
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "NET TOPLAM")
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, nettoplam)
	End If

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 15, "%" & kdvc & "KDV")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, kdvtutar)

	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1+1, 5, 39, String(22, "-"))
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "G.TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, geneltoplam)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")


	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	SS = 4.3
	SSB = 225
	res = pe1.write(SSB, solmargin + 10, 10, 150, not1)
	res = pe1.write(SSB + SS, solmargin + 10, 10, 150, not2)
	res = pe1.write(SSB + 2 * SS, solmargin + 10, 10, 150, not3)
	res = pe1.write(SSB + 3 * SS, solmargin + 10, 10, 200, "Yalnýz " & Doc.numtotext_TL(geneltoplam))

End Sub

Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub

Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function

Sub AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi,solFiyat+2,5,genFiyat,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar+2, 5, genTutar, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,2))	
End Sub




Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(4,0) = "TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0,1) = 35
	Grid(1,1) = 65
	Grid(2,1) = 25
	Grid(3,1) = 23
	Grid(4,1) = 35
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK70T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(4,6) = 2
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, AraToplam, sonSayfasatirSonu
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	
	AraToplam = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 275
	sayfaEni = 213
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)

	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 70
	satirBaslangic = 85
	satirBaslangic = 107.5
	sonSayfasatirSonu = satirSonu - DipToplamSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi

	ustmargin = 15
	solmargin = 11
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK70T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	chkodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi  = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	adres1 = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
	adres2 = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
	adres3 = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	vergidairesi = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK70E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = doc.strtonordate(STOK70E.TARIH(1))
	satirSayisi = STOK70T.GetRecCount()

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	bBasla = ustmargin + 32
	res=pe1.write(bBasla, solmargin + 10, 5, 100, chadi)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 10, 5, 150, adres1)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 10, 5, 150, adres2)
	res=pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 10, 5, 150, adres3)

' *************************************************************************
'	Nitelik = STOK70E.NITELIK
'	if trim(Nitelik) = "SR" then
'		res = pe1.Setalign("CENTER")
'		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+37, solmargin, 5, sagmargin - solMargin, "ALIM ÝADE FATURASI")		
'		res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
'		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	end if
' *************************************************************************

	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 59, solmargin + 10, 5, 50, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 59, solmargin + 60, 5, 50, vergino)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 28, solmargin +156, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)


	For k = 1 To satirsayisi
		irsaliyeno = STOK70T.IRSALIYENO(k)
		irsaliyetarihi = doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeno, "")
		irsaliyetarihi = doc.strtonordate(irsaliyetarihi)
				irsaliyeno1 = STOK70T.IRSALIYENO(1)
		if k=1 then
		res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 49, solmargin + 145, 5, 20, irsaliyetarihi)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 49, solmargin + 165, 5, 40, irsaliyeno1)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
		d = 1
		else
			if irsaliyeno <> irsaliyeno1 then
				d = d + 1
				irsaliyeno1 = irsaliyeno
				res = pe1.write(ustmargin+49+(d-1)*4, solmargin + 145, 5, 40, irsaliyetarihi)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				res = pe1.write(ustmargin+49+(d-1)*4, solmargin + 165, 5, 40, irsaliyeno)
				res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
			end if
		end if
	Next
	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
' **************************************************************
		If trim(STOK70T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

'		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
'			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'			siraNo = 1
'			res = pe1.PageBreak()
'			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
'			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
'		Else
'			If satirNo = 1 Then
'					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
'			Else
'					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
'			End If
'			siraNo = siraNo + 1
'		End If		
' ************************************************************************

		RSATIR = STOK70T.GetRecCount()
		If sonSatir >=  satirSonu or RSATIR and sonSatir >= sonSayfasatirSonu Then
			Call AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
				satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.setbold(0)

		if pe1.GetPageCount() > 1 and sirano = 1 then 
			res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr , SOLMARGIN + 130, satirYuksekligi, 20, "NAKLÝ YEKÜN") 
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, SOLMARGIN + 145, satirYuksekligi, 39, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,2)) 		
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			satirpozr = satirpozr + satirYuksekligi
			sirano = sirano + 1
		end if

		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = solMargin + 5

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				stokkodu = STOK70T.KOD(satirno)
				mstokkodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
				mstokadi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))

				if degiskenAdi = "TL_TUTAR" then
					TT = STOK70T.TL_TUTAR(SatirNo)
'					AraToplam = AraToplam + STOK70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
					AraToplam = AraToplam + TT
				end if

				If trim(degiskenAdi) = "KOD" Then
						If trim(mstokkodu) <> "" Then
								Degisken = trim(mstokkodu) 
						End If
				End If
				If trim(degiskenAdi) = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
						If trim(mstokadi) <> "" Then
								Degisken = trim(mstokadi) 
						End If
				End If
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.Numformat(degisken, 20, Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				if i=0 or i=1 then
					res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				else
					res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
				end if
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				xCizgi(j) =	Sol - 2
				j = j + 1
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub


Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
		
		For i = 0 To 3
			SutunG = Grid(i,1)
			Sol1 = Sol1 + SutunG
		Next
	Ondalik = Grid(4,6)

	Toplam = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM(1), 39, Ondalik)
	iskontotoplam = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1), 39, Ondalik)

	nettoplam = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1),39,Ondalik)
	KDVOrani = STOK70T.KDV_ORANI(1)
	KOndalik = Doc.SigDig(KDVOrani)
	kdvc = doc.Numformat(KDVOrani,7, KOndalik)

	kdvtutar = doc.Numformat(STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM(1),39,Ondalik)
	geneltoplam = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	geneltoplam = doc.Numformat(geneltoplam,39,Ondalik)
	not1 = STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3(1)

	sol1 = sol1 - 4
	satirs = 219

'	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs, sol1, 5, 39, iskontotoplam)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+8, sol1, 5, 39, nettoplam)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+16, sol1-10, 5, 10, kdvc)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+16, sol1, 5, 39, kdvtutar)
'	res = pe1.write(satirs+24, sol1, 5, 39, geneltoplam)
'	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

' QQQ
	sol1 = sol1 + 12
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1+1, 5, 39, String(22, "-"))
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, Toplam)

	If iskontotoplam <> "" Then
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "ÝSKONTO")
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, iskontotoplam)
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1+1, 5, 39, String(22, "-"))
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "NET TOPLAM")
			res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, nettoplam)
	End If

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 15, "%" & kdvc & "KDV")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, kdvtutar)

	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1+1, 5, 39, String(22, "-"))
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sol1-12, 5, 25, "G.TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, sol1, 5, 39, geneltoplam)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")


	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
	SS = 4.3
	SSB = 225
	res = pe1.write(SSB, solmargin + 10, 10, 150, "3065 Sayýlý KDV Kanunu Hükümleri Gereðince Satýldýðýndan ")
	res = pe1.write(SSB + SS, solmargin + 10, 10, 150, "KDV Tahsil edilmemiþtir")
'	res = pe1.write(SSB + 2 * SS, solmargin + 10, 10, 150, not3)
	res = pe1.write(SSB + 3 * SS, solmargin + 10, 10, 200, "Yalnýz " & Doc.numtotext_TL(geneltoplam))

End Sub

Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 8)
		res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub

Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function

Sub AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	for i = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,0) = "TL_TUTAR" then
			GenTutar = Grid(i,1) - 2
			solTutar = Sol1			
		elseif Grid(i,0) = "TL_FIYAT" then
			GenFiyat = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1) - 2
			solFiyat = Sol1
		end if
		sol1 = sol1 + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi,solFiyat+2,5,genFiyat,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar+2, 5, genTutar, Doc.NumFormat(AraToplam,20,2))	
End Sub




Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	Grid(4,0) = "TL_TUTAR"

	Grid(0,1) = 35
	Grid(1,1) = 65
	Grid(2,1) = 25
	Grid(3,1) = 23
	Grid(4,1) = 35
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK70T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(4,6) = 2
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1,imlt00

	Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res=pe1.Open(0,"Kapasite Ýhtiyaç Planý",-1,-1,"")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.6)

   Set imlt00 = doc.GetTableObject("IMLT00")
	Call xxx(pe1,imlt00)
	Call yyy(pe1,imlt00)	
	Call zzz(pe1,imlt00)	
	
	res=pe1.Show()
	res=doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub xxx(pe1,imlt00)
	donemSayisi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","GERIVEILERIKACDONEME",-1)
	donemAraligi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","BIRDONEMKACGUN",-1)	
	baslangicTarihi = doc.strtonordate(doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","HESAPLAMATARIHI",-1))
	chartBaslik = trim(imlt00.getfieldValue("AD",0))
	bQYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",3)
	bCYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",5)
	bPYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",6)
	bRYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",7)
	bKapasite = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",1)
	Statu = doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","STATULER",-1)
	Statu = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("|SELS1","AD",Statu,-1))
	donemSayisi = 23

	veri=pe1.ChartDataCreate(2,donemSayisi)
	res=pe1.SetChartTitle("Kapasite Ýhtiyaç Planý")
	res=pe1.SetChartSubTitle(chartBaslik)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootNoteFontSizeFactor(0.8)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootnote("(Dönem Aralýðý : " & donemAraligi & " gün, Baþlangýç Tarihi : " & baslangicTarihi & ")")	
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri,0,rgb(32,208,40))
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri,1,rgb(0,0,255))
'	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri,2,rgb(255,0,0))
'	res=pe1.ChartDataSetLineStyle(veri,2,1)
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetXAxisTitle(Veri,"Dönemler")
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetYAxisTitle(Veri,"(Saat)")
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,bKapasite)	
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,"Bekleyen Ýþler " & Statu)	
'	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,bKalanHmmdSt)	
	res=pe1.SetChartLegendPosition(7)
	res=pe1.SetChartXLabelStartValue(1)			

	For i= 67 To 89
		Kapasite = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),1)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri,0,Kapasite)			
'		x = x + DonemlikHmdTalebi
	Next
	For i= 66 To 89
'		QYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),3)
'		CYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),5)
'		PYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),6)
'		RYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),7)
'		ToplamYuk = QYuk + CYuk + PYuk + RYuk
		ToplamYuk = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),8)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri,1,ToplamYuk)			
'		y=y+AcikSatinalmaSip
	Next
	
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgColor(rgb(255,255,220))
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgMode(0)
	res=pe1.Chart(10,10, 85,190,veri,2)	
End Sub

Sub yyy(pe1,imlt00)
	donemSayisi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","GERIVEILERIKACDONEME",-1)
	donemAraligi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","BIRDONEMKACGUN",-1)	
	baslangicTarihi = doc.strtonordate(doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","HESAPLAMATARIHI",-1))
	chartBaslik = trim(imlt00.getfieldValue("AD",0))
	bKumKapasite = trim(doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",2))
	bKumTalep = trim(doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",9))
	Statu = doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","STATULER",-1)
	Statu = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("|SELS1","AD",Statu,-1))
	donemSayisi = 23

	veri2=pe1.ChartDataCreate(2,donemSayisi)
	res=pe1.SetChartTitle("Kümülatif Yük / Kümülatif Kapasite")
	res=pe1.SetChartSubTitle(chartBaslik)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootNoteFontSizeFactor(0.8)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootnote("(Dönem Aralýðý : " & donemAraligi & " gün, Baþlangýç Tarihi : " & baslangicTarihi & ")")	
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri2,0,rgb(32,208,40))
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri2,1,rgb(0,0,255))
'	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri2,2,rgb(255,0,0))
'	res=pe1.ChartDataSetLineStyle(veri2,2,1)
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetXAxisTitle(Veri2,"Dönemler")
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetYAxisTitle(Veri2,"(Saat)")
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri2,bKumKapasite)	
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri2,bKumTalep & " - " & Statu)	
'	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri,bKalanHmmdSt)	
	res=pe1.SetChartLegendPosition(7)
	res=pe1.SetChartXLabelStartValue(1)			

	For i= 66 To 89
		KumKapasite = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),2)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri2,0,KumKapasite)			
	Next
	For i= 66 To 89
		KumTalep = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),9)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri2,1,KumTalep)			
	Next
	
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgColor(rgb(255,255,220))
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgMode(0)
	res=pe1.Chart(102,10, 85,190,veri2,1)	
End Sub

Sub zzz(pe1,imlt00)
	donemSayisi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","GERIVEILERIKACDONEME",-1)
	donemAraligi= doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","BIRDONEMKACGUN",-1)	
	baslangicTarihi = doc.strtonordate(doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","HESAPLAMATARIHI",-1))
	chartBaslik = trim(imlt00.getfieldValue("AD",0))
	bDolulukYuzdesi = trim(doc.getfieldvalue("DT03","A",13))
	Statu = doc.getfieldvalue("STACK","STATULER",-1)
	Statu = trim(doc.GetGecoValue("|SELS1","AD",Statu,-1))
	donemSayisi = 23

	veri3=pe1.ChartDataCreate(1,donemSayisi)
	res=pe1.SetChartTitle(bDolulukYuzdesi)
	res=pe1.SetChartSubTitle(chartBaslik)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootNoteFontSizeFactor(0.8)
	res=pe1.SetChartFootnote("(Dönem Aralýðý : " & donemAraligi & " gün, Baþlangýç Tarihi : " & baslangicTarihi & ")")	
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri3,0,rgb(0,0,255))
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetXAxisTitle(veri3,"Dönemler")
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetYAxisTitle(veri3,"(%)")
	res=pe1.ChartDataAddLegend(veri3,bDolulukYuzdesi & " - " & statu)	
	res=pe1.SetChartLegendPosition(7)
	res=pe1.SetChartXLabelStartValue(1)			

	For i= 67 To 89
		DolulukYuzdesi = doc.getfieldvalue("DT03",chr(i),13)
		res=pe1.ChartDataAddValue(veri3,0,DolulukYuzdesi)			
	Next
	
	res=pe1.ChartDataSetColor(veri3,0,rgb(32,208,40))
	res=pe1.SetChartGridBkgMode(0)
	res=pe1.Chart(195,10, 85,190,veri3,1)	
End Sub
